### [1] [TAPe+ML: A Compact Structured Representation for Multi-Task Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20869)
*Sergey Kurinov,Alexey Upatov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAPe+ML v3，一种基于主动感知理论（TAPe）结构化表示的紧凑计算机视觉系统，在不足10万参数下实现分类、检测、分割多任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉模型直接处理像素张量，依赖大量网络参数，导致数据、内存与计算需求高。作者希望将部分建模负担从网络参数转移到结构化的输入表示，从而构建紧凑的多任务视觉系统。

Method: 基于TAPe（Theory of Active Perception）构建在识别前编码感知元素间关系的共享结构化表示，避免直接操作像素张量。系统采用模块化识别架构，结合背景与轮廓处理、局部目标定位、基于原型的分类，以及用于协调专用子模型的协调器，统一支持图像分类、目标检测和实例分割。

Result: 参数量少于10万。COCO目标检测达84.7 mAP50、65.3 mAP50-95；COCO实例分割达80.7 mask mAP50、58.4 mask mAP50-95。Imagenette在相同训练对比下验证准确率92%（对比raw-pixel基线），ImageNet-Real Top-1准确率89.9%。并在视频场景检测与工业试点分布偏移适应中评估了紧凑性。

Conclusion: 将部分建模负担从网络参数转移到结构化输入表示，可支持具有更低数据、内存和计算需求的紧凑多任务视觉系统。

Abstract: We present TAPe+ML v3, a compact computer vision system based on TAPe (Theory of Active Perception), a structured representation that encodes relations among perceptual elements before recognition. Instead of operating directly on pixel tensors, the system uses a shared TAPe representation and a modular recognition architecture for image classification, object detection, and instance segmentation.
  TAPe+ML v3 combines background and contour processing, local object localization, prototype-based classification, and a coordinator for specialized submodels. Across the reported experiments, it uses fewer than 100,000 parameters. On COCO object detection, it obtains 84.7 mAP50 and 65.3 mAP50-95. On COCO instance segmentation, it obtains 80.7 mask mAP50 and 58.4 mask mAP50-95. In classification experiments, it reaches 92 percent validation accuracy on Imagenette under an identical-training comparison with a raw-pixel baseline, and 89.9 percent Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-Real. We also evaluate compactness in video scene detection and adaptation under distribution shift in an industrial pilot. The results suggest that shifting part of the modeling burden from network parameters to a structured input representation can support compact multi-task vision systems with reduced data, memory, and compute requirements.

</details>


### [2] [MemeTAG: Keyword-Driven Meme Classification through Tag Embedding Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20962)
*Akshit Sharma,Prashant W. Patil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MemeTAG，一种面向有害网络模因分类的双目标框架，通过关键词感知与语义引导机制提升多模态分类性能，在多个数据集上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有害网络模因传播带来显著社会威胁，但其内容具有细微且多模态的特性，自动化分类仍是严峻的算法挑战。

Method: 提出 MemeTAG 双目标框架：先用预训练视觉-语言模型生成描述性关键词以捕捉高层语义；再用基于注意力的聚合标签推断网络（ATIN）将关键词蒸馏为单一丰富语义嵌入，并将其作为辅助重建损失目标，促使模型学习深度对齐的视觉与文本特征；同时采用高效三阶段训练策略。

Result: 在 HarMeme、Hateful Memes Challenge（HMC）和 PrideMM 数据集上取得新的最优性能，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 关键词感知的语义引导与重建损失相结合，可有效提升有害模因的多模态分类效果，验证了该框架的有效性与先进性。

Abstract: The proliferation of harmful internet memes poses a significant societal threat, yet their automated classification remains a formidable algorithmic challenge due to the nuanced, multimodal nature of their content. To address this, we introduce MemeTAG, a novel dual-objective framework that pioneers a keyword-aware approach to meme classification. Our core innovation is a two-part semantic guidance mechanism: first, we leverage a pretrained Vision-Language Model to generate a set of descriptive keywords, that capture the high-level semantics. Second, we introduce the Aggregated Tag Inference Network (ATIN), an attention-based module that distills these keywords into a single, rich semantic embedding. This embedding serves as a target for a novel auxiliary reconstruction loss, which compels the model to learn deeply aligned visual and textual features. This approach, combined with an efficient three-stage training strategy, establishes a new state-of-the-art on the HarMeme, Hateful Memes Challenge (HMC), and PrideMM datasets, decisively outperforming existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [3] [Image-Derived PM10 Estimation in Cattle Feedlot Using Machine Learning: Addressing Concentration Ranges Beyond Existing Digital Imaging Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20975)
*Sirapoom Peanusaha,Greg B. Ferguson,K. Jack Bush,Peiyang Li,Brent W. Auvermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种基于对比面板特征与机器学习的图像方法，在商业化牛饲养场估算PM10浓度（小时均值250–1000 μg/m³，瞬时可达5000–20000 μg/m³），XGBoost表现最佳（R²=0.792，中位绝对误差103 μg/m³）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛饲养场行业亟需低成本粉尘监测手段，但基于摄像头的PM估算研究此前主要集中于城市空气质量场景，尚未在集约化畜牧作业所特有的大幅浓度范围下得到验证。

Method: 在商业化牛饲养场傍晚粉尘高峰时段拍摄灰度图像，提取面板对比度、黑白面板像素值、整体图像亮度等特征，并引入前序图像的近期历史值与太阳天顶角作为预测因子，使用多种机器学习模型（含XGBoost）进行PM10浓度估算。

Result: XGBoost预测性能最优，R²为0.792，中位绝对误差103 μg/m³。特征重要性分析显示：距相机最远的面板对预测贡献最大，且黑色面板像素值比白色面板对PM10变化更敏感。日落过渡期（恰逢饲养场傍晚粉尘高峰起始）的预测精度仍需改进。

Conclusion: 研究证明图像法PM10估算可适用于远超以往城市研究报道的浓度范围，并为未来在饲养场环境中部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Affordable dust monitoring remains a pressing need for the cattle feedlot industry, yet camera-based PM estimation, despite its growing body of research in urban air quality settings, has not been evaluated under the extended concentration ranges characteristic of intensive livestock operations. This study developed an image-based approach using contrast panel features and machine learning to estimate PM10 concentrations in a commercial cattle feedlot, where hourly average PM10 ranged from 250 to 1,000 ug/m^-3 and instantaneous concentrations reached 5,000 to 20,000 ug/m^-3. Grayscale images were captured during the evening dust peak period, and features including panel contrast, black and white panel pixel values, and overall image brightness were extracted. The model also incorporated recent past values from preceding images and solar zenith angle as predictors. Among the candidate models evaluated, XGBoost achieved the highest predictive performance, with an R^2 of 0.792 and a median absolute error of 103 ug/m^-3. Feature importance analysis revealed that (a) panels positioned farthest from the camera contributed most strongly to predictions and (b) that black panel pixel values were more sensitive than white panel values to changes in PM10 concentration. Prediction accuracy during the sunset transition, which coincides with the onset of the feedlot evening dust peak, remains an area for further refinement. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of image-based PM10 estimation across PM concentration ranges substantially exceeding those reported in prior urban studies and provide practical guidelines for future deployment in feedlot environments.

</details>


### [4] [Fragment-Aware Vision Transformers for Fresco-Fragment Style Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21012)
*Sara Miketek,Biagio Barchielli,Nadeem Iqbal Kajla,Sinem Aslan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究壁画残片（片段）的艺术风格分类，提出渐进式Transformer框架，通过前景引导掩码、修复式几何正则化与分布级对比学习改进ViT基线，并证明简单集成优于复杂图融合，同时给出可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有艺术风格分类多基于完整艺术品，可利用全局构图与空间结构；而在考古场景中作品常仅存碎片，识别只能依赖不完整、不规则且缺乏上下文的视觉证据，因此需要专门面向残片的建模方法。

Method: 在ViT-B/16基线之上逐步引入三项改进：(1) 前景引导掩码，抑制纯背景token；(2) 基于图像修复（inpainting）的几何正则化，使不规则残片支撑与ViT的patch网格对齐；(3) 监督对比目标，在预测分布层面通过KL相似度构造，对每个分支均有一致提升。各分支用简单的可学习logit集成进行融合，并与更复杂的图融合变体做对比。

Result: 在CLEOPATRA上，集成将准确率从0.604提升至0.656，macro-F1从0.596提升至0.648；在POMPAAF上，集成在六种碎片化设置中的四种优于最佳单分支。复杂图融合变体在POMPAAF上与简单集成持平，在CLEOPATRA仅有小幅且依赖数据集的增益，不足以证明其额外复杂度合理。可解释性分析表明模型利用了真实绘画证据，但也量化出修复分支的部分归因来自合成的周边区域。

Conclusion: 面向残片的建模优于标准ViT基线，简单logit集成通常足够且更简洁；两项核心贡献为分布级对比目标能稳定锐化单分支识别，以及验证并量化模型证据来源的可解释性分析。

Abstract: Artistic style classification is usually studied on complete artworks, where models can exploit global composition, spatial organisation, and iconographic structure. In archaeological settings, however, artworks often survive only as fragmented remains, forcing recognition from incomplete, irregular, and context-limited visual evidence. We study fresco-fragment style classification using a progressive transformer-based framework. Starting from a ViT-B/16 baseline, we introduce foreground-guided masking to suppress background-only tokens, inpainting-based geometric regularisation to align irregular fragment supports with the ViT patch grid, and a supervised contrastive objective that operates on predictive distributions through a Kullback-Leibler similarity and consistently improves every branch. We combine the branches with a deliberately simple learnable logit ensemble. Experiments on CLEOPATRA and POMPAAF show that fragment-aware modelling improves over the standard ViT baseline, with the ensemble increasing accuracy from 0.604 to 0.656 and macro-F1 from 0.596 to 0.648 on CLEOPATRA, and outperforming the best single branch in four of six fragmentation settings on POMPAAF. We additionally evaluate a more complex graph-fusion variant and find that it matches the simple ensemble on POMPAAF while offering only a small, dataset-specific gain on CLEOPATRA, which does not justify its added complexity. Beyond these empirical gains, our contribution is twofold: a distribution-level contrastive objective that consistently sharpens single-branch recognition, and an interpretability analysis that verifies the models exploit genuine painted evidence, while quantifying that the inpainting-based branch draws part of its attribution from the synthesised surround.

</details>


### [5] [MAGIC: Marginal-Guided Compression with Optimal Transport for Efficient Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21018)
*Xu Yuan,Hua Liu,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的后处理压缩器MAGIC，通过双边缘熵最优传输对齐MaxSim检索需求，缓解激进压缩下多向量视觉文档检索的索引存储与评分开销问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ColPali等视觉文档检索系统采用多向量页面嵌入，patch级向量支持细粒度证据匹配，但带来巨大的索引存储和MaxSim评分开销。后处理合并是免重训练的高效途径，但其均匀重构目标与晚期交互检索诱导的稀疏、非均匀patch使用不匹配；激进压缩下会保留少用patch，而检索活动集中于过少保留代表。

Method: MAGIC是一种面向冻结多向量嵌入的免训练后处理压缩器。它推导MaxSim诱导的压缩代理目标，并通过双边缘熵最优传输公式优化：检索需求源边缘优先高使用patch，平衡目标边缘正则化保留facet的使用。

Result: 在ViDoRe基准、多种保留率和检索骨干网络上，MAGIC持续优于强后处理压缩器，在激进压缩情形下增益尤为显著；组件消融验证了双边缘的互补作用。

Conclusion: MAGIC通过最优传输的双边缘设计有效对齐晚期交互检索的稀疏patch使用，实现免训练、强鲁棒的高效多向量视觉文档检索压缩，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent visual document retrieval (VDR) systems such as ColPali use multi-vector page embeddings, in which patch-level vectors enable fine-grained evidence matching but incur substantial index storage and MaxSim scoring overhead. Post-hoc merging offers a practical route to efficient VDR by reducing this cost without retraining the retriever, but its uniform reconstruction objectives are poorly aligned with the sparse, non-uniform patch usage induced by late-interaction retrieval. Under aggressive compression, this misalignment can preserve rarely used patches while concentrating retrieval activity on too few retained representatives. To address this misalignment, we propose Marginal-Guided Compression with Optimal Transport (MAGIC), a training-free post-hoc compressor for efficient retrieval with frozen multi-vector embeddings. MAGIC derives a MaxSim-induced compression surrogate and optimizes it through a two-marginal entropic optimal-transport formulation, where a retrieval-demand source marginal prioritizes high-use patches and a balanced target marginal regularizes retained-facet usage. Across ViDoRe benchmarks, keep ratios, and retrieval backbones, MAGIC consistently outperforms strong post-hoc compressors, with particularly large gains in the aggressive-compression regime; component ablations verify the complementary effects of its two marginals. We release the code at: https://github.com/xandery-geek/MAGIC.

</details>


### [6] [MarsFM: Shading-Regularized Flow Matching for Martian Relief Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21095)
*Marius F. R. Juston*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MarsFM是一个图像条件的潜在流匹配模型，用于从单波段HiRISE RED正射影像估计火星局部地形起伏，结合生成先验、立体几何监督与可微Lunar-Lambert光照目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单波段火星影像估计局部地形起伏具有挑战性，需要结合学习到的地形先验与图像约束，以在缺乏独立高分辨率参考数据的情况下推进物理地形分辨率。

Method: 采用图像条件的潜在流匹配模型，结合预训练生成先验、立体派生的几何监督和可微Lunar-Lambert光照目标；使用起伏、法向量、梯度、曲率及序数项约束地形结构，并用正仿射不变图像比较约束渲染外观。

Result: 在2024个补丁记录上评估，1至20步欧拉积分的归一化signed-log起伏空间中，平均仿射对齐RMSE在0.0935至0.0957之间；空间、微分与光谱诊断显示地形总体对应，但存在平滑、振幅压缩和边界不匹配。

Conclusion: MarsFM为结合学习地形先验与图像约束提供了框架；改进物理地形分辨率仍需匹配的基线和独立高分辨率参考数据。

Abstract: We present MarsFM, an image-conditioned latent flow-matching model for local Martian relief estimation from single-band HiRISE RED orthoimagery. The method combines a pretrained generative prior with stereo-derived geometric supervision and a differentiable Lunar--Lambert shading objective. Relief, normal, gradient, curvature, and ordinal terms constrain complementary aspects of terrain structure, while a positive-affine-invariant image comparison constrains rendered appearance. An evaluation comprising 2024 gathered patch records per integration-step count yields mean affine-aligned RMSE between 0.0935 and 0.0957 in normalized signed-log relief space for one to twenty Euler steps. These scores measure agreement with VAE-reconstructed references on positive-reference support. Their narrow range supports low-step inference under this protocol. Spatial, differential, and spectral diagnostics show broad terrain correspondence alongside smoothing, amplitude compression, and boundary mismatch. MarsFM provides a framework for combining learned terrain priors with image-based constraints; establishing improved physical terrain resolution requires matched baselines and independent high-resolution reference data. Data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/SuperComputer/mars_hirise_dtm_processed-6aa9b66ba461e07f; code: https://github.com/Marius-Juston/MarsRecon.

</details>


### [7] [4DGS-Fixer: Generative Sparse-View 4D Gaussian Splatting with Iterative Refinement Guided by Video Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21176)
*Haitao Huang,Shenghao Zhao,Boyuan Tian,Shin-Fang Chng,Songlin Yang,Sheila Lim,Huangying Zhan,Yi Xu,Anyi Rao,Frank Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于视频扩散模型的迭代精炼框架，解决稀疏视角4D高斯建模中几何初始化不全与观测不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角视频的动态场景合成中，现有几何先验、自适应优化或密度控制方法无法根本解决观测不足导致的病态问题；且稀疏输入使COLMAP重建的点云稀疏不完整，大区域缺乏高斯支撑，后续优化难以恢复。

Method: 先估计多视角深度图并融合为稠密点云，为动态4DGS提供更完整几何初始化；再用预训练视频修复模型，对不同时间步新视角轨迹的渲染序列进行精炼，并以修复序列作为伪监督，正则化并迭代精炼4DGS表示。

Result: 在常用基准数据集上显著优于现有基线，相较此前最佳方法PSNR提升近2 dB。

Conclusion: 通过稠密几何初始化与视频扩散伪监督的迭代精炼，可有效提升稀疏视角动态4D场景的完整性与一致性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenges of dynamic scene synthesis from sparse-view videos. Existing methods employ geometric priors, adaptive optimization, or density-control strategies to improve 4D Gaussian modeling under sparse observations. However, they cannot fundamentally resolve the ill-posed problem caused by insufficient observations and missing scene information. Moreover, sparse-view 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) often suffers from poor geometric initialization: with only a few input views, COLMAP typically reconstructs sparse and incomplete point clouds, leaving large scene regions without sufficient Gaussian support and making them difficult to recover through subsequent optimization. To address these limitations, we propose a novel iterative refinement framework based on a video diffusion model to improve the completeness and consistency of dynamic 4D scenes. Specifically, we first estimate multi-view depth maps and fuse them into dense point clouds to provide more complete geometric initialization for a dynamic 4DGS representation. We then employ a pretrained video restoration model to refine sequences rendered along novel camera trajectories at different time steps. The restored sequences serve as pseudo-supervision to regularize and iteratively refine the 4DGS representation. Experiments on a widely used benchmark dataset demonstrate that our method substantially outperforms existing baselines, achieving nearly a 2 dB PSNR improvement over the previous best-performing method.

</details>


### [8] [OnomatoBridge: Onomatopoeia Translation and Rendering Pipeline in Manga](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21199)
*Takara Taniguchi,Wataru Shimoda,Kota Yamaguchi,Hideki Nakayama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OnomatoBridge，一种用于漫画视觉拟声词翻译的过滤流水线，实现日译英拟声词替换，改善文本正确性并减少残留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漫画拟声词具有独特的视觉风格，传达声音、动作与情感。现有视觉拟声词翻译方法在去除日文拟声词并渲染风格化英文时，常产生残留伪影或风格不一致问题。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 提出 OnomatoBridge，一种用于视觉拟声词翻译的过滤流水线，在 Manga109 拟声词数据集上进行日译英评估，并与基线图像编辑模型进行比较。

Result: 实验结果显示，经所提方法过滤后的输出优于传统方法：英文文本正确性提升约 10 至 25 个百分点，日文残留文本相对减少约 20% 至 50%。

Conclusion: OnomatoBridge 能有效改善漫画视觉拟声词翻译质量，在提升英文正确性的同时显著减少日文残留。

Abstract: Manga is a comic drawn by black and white paints gaining popularity around the world. Onomatopoeia in Manga specifically appeals to the audience with its unique visual styles, which convey sound, motion, and emotion. Visual onomatopoeia translation requires the clean replacement of Japanese onomatopoeia with onomatopoeia in the other language while preserving their visual style. Existing approaches often produce residual artifacts or style inconsistency when removing the Japanese onomatopoeia and rendering stylized English onomatopoeia. To approach these problems, we present OnomatoBridge, a filtering pipeline for visual onomatopoeia translation. We evaluate OnomatoBridge from Japanese to English on the Manga109 onomatopoeia dataset and compare it with baseline image editing models. Experimental results show that the filtered outputs by the proposed method outperform those of conventional methods. OnomatoBridge improves English text correctness by roughly 10 to 25 points and reduces residual Japanese text by about 20 to 50% in relative terms.

</details>


### [9] [Hand-Aware Transition Modeling for Bimanual Procedural Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21207)
*Di Wen,Jimmy Weissert,Luc Maria Scherrer,Cedric Zöllner,Kailun Yang,Ruiping Liu,Yufan Chen,Jiale Wei,Junwei Zheng,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 HACT，一个基于预测的双手事件转移模型，用于双手装配中的程序性异常检测，通过角色保持历史与标记时序点过程计算语义和时间意外度，并结合监督证据头与双状态滤波输出每手异常后验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手装配中的程序性异常检测需要将每只手的动作与已有执行过程对照判断。某些纠正性动作单独看可能显得异常，而视觉上合理的动作却可能违反流程顺序，因此仅凭孤立动作难以判别异常。

Method: HACT 对预测的每手事件建立转移模型。方法包含：角色保持历史以保留双手并发的职责信息；标记时序点过程为每个观测转移赋予语义与时间意外度；监督证据头与双状态滤波器将意外度转换为每手异常后验。并在预测事件上采用恢复感知协议与参与者不相交折，报告在验证参与者上选定工作点的恢复假阳性率。

Result: 在两个双手电动工具流程上，HACT 在所比较方法中取得最高 AUPRC 与 F1，且恢复报警最少。在不重训练的情况下迁移到同一产品的不同装配顺序时，仍保持最高 AUPRC 与 F1。

Conclusion: HACT 能有效建模双手装配中的程序性异常，兼顾语义与时间意外度，并在跨装配顺序场景下保持领先性能与更低的恢复误报。源代码已开源于 https://github.com/Kratos-Wen/HACT。

Abstract: Procedural anomaly detection in bimanual assembly requires judging each hand action against the execution so far. A corrective action may look unusual in isolation, while a visually plausible action can violate the order of the procedure. We present HACT, a transition model over predicted per-hand events. A role-preserving history keeps the concurrent responsibilities of both hands, and a marked temporal point process assigns each observed transition a semantic and temporal surprisal. A supervised evidence head and a two-state filter convert these surprisals into per-hand anomaly posteriors. A recovery-aware protocol on predicted events and participant-disjoint folds reports the recovery false-positive rate at an operating point selected on validation participants. On two bimanual power-tool procedures HACT has the highest AUPRC and F1 among the compared methods and the fewest recovery alarms. Applied without retraining to a different assembly order of the same product, it retains the highest AUPRC and F1. The source code is available at https://github.com/Kratos-Wen/HACT.

</details>


### [10] [Multi-viewpoint Geo-localization with Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21219)
*Adam D. Hines,Michael Milford,Tobias Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MegaEvent——一种具备视角鲁棒性的事件相机视觉位置识别系统，通过将5个大规模数据集转为合成事件流微调预训练事件ViT，在多个数据集上大幅领先现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人定位需要容忍视角变化的建图与定位系统。事件相机在机器人领域日益受关注，但现有事件定位器对视角变化问题的研究不足，且强调视角变化的挑战性事件数据集稀缺。

Method: 将5个常用于训练帧基定位系统的大规模地理标注数据集，通过Image-to-Event（I2E）转换生成合成事件流；用多损失函数微调预训练的事件视觉Transformer骨干，得到MegaEvent，学习视角鲁棒的位置识别特征；并构建新数据集Springfield-Event-VPR（3.7km步行路线、三种相机朝向、共11.1km）。

Result: 在三个现有事件定位数据集上平均Recall@1达82%，领先次优事件方法20个召回点，领先直接应用于事件帧的帧基VPR模型8~26个召回点；在新数据集Springfield-Event-VPR上比最强基线高9个召回点。

Conclusion: MegaEvent在视角变化下实现鲁棒的事件基视觉位置识别，显著超越现有事件与帧基方法，并提供了新的挑战性基准数据集，代码已开源。

Abstract: Robot localization is an ongoing challenge that demands mapping and positioning systems that are tolerant to viewpoint change. Event cameras are attracting increasing interest and adoption in robotics; however, dealing with viewpoint variance is an under-investigated problem in existing event-based localizers. In addition, event-based datasets that emphasize viewpoint variance for challenging localization situations are scarce. Here, we introduce an event-based visual place recognition (VPR) system that performs robustly under viewpoint changes. We converted five large-scale geo-tagged datasets, conventionally used to train frame-based localization systems, into synthetic event streams using Image-to-Event (I2E) conversion, and used them to fine-tune a pre-trained event-based vision transformer backbone with a multi-loss function, yielding a system we call MegaEvent that learns viewpoint-robust features for place recognition. We achieved an average Recall@1 of 82% across three existing event-based localization datasets, leading the next best event-based method by 20 recall points, and frame-based VPR models applied directly to event frames by 8 to 26 recall points. We introduce a new, challenging dataset - Springfield-Event-VPR - which features a 3.7km walking route recorded in three camera orientations for a total of 11.1km, which MegaEvent outperforms the strongest baseline by 9 recall points. The code for MegaEvent is available at https://github.com/AdamDHines/megaevent.

</details>


### [11] [VGGT-CAD: Reconstructing Parametric CAD 3D Model with Geometric Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21225)
*Chunan Yu,Tianrun Chen,Fu Shen,Cheng Chen,Lanyun Zhu,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VGGT-CAD，一个几何感知的参数化CAD重建框架，可从单视图或多视图观测中恢复精确几何与可编辑建模操作，并构建了多视图视频基准VideoCAD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化CAD重建需从视觉观测中同时恢复精确几何和可编辑建模操作，在视图有限且存在歧义时尤为困难。现有方法主要依赖2D外观线索，缺乏强有力的多视图几何先验。

Method: 将预训练3D几何先验迁移到CAD重建中，把相机参数编码为条件token并与图像token联合建模；提出可变视图跨视图上下文聚合模块以自适应融合多视图特征；设计免训练的几何感知视图选择策略在推理时挑选互补且可靠的帧；使用非自回归解码器将表示解码为CAD命令序列；并构建大规模多视图视频基准VideoCAD。

Result: 大量实验表明，VGGT-CAD在不同观测配置下的视觉CAD重建中具有有效性。

Conclusion: 几何感知的多视图先验与自适应融合可有效提升参数化CAD重建性能，VideoCAD基准为该任务提供了多视图评测支持。

Abstract: Parametric CAD reconstruction requires recovering both precise geometry and editable modeling operations from visual observations, making it challenging under limited and ambiguous views. Existing methods mainly rely on 2D appearance cues and lack strong multi-view geometric priors. In this work, we present VGGT-CAD, a geometry-aware framework for parametric CAD reconstruction from single- and multi-view observations. We transfer pretrained 3D geometric priors into CAD reconstruction by encoding camera parameters as condition tokens and jointly modeling them with image tokens. To handle varying numbers of viewpoints, we introduce a variable-view cross-view context aggregation module that adaptively fuses multi-view features. We further develop a training-free geometry-aware view selection strategy to select complementary and reliable frames during inference. The resulting representation is decoded into CAD command sequences using a non-autoregressive decoder. We also develop VideoCAD, a large-scale multi-view video benchmark derived from existing CAD data through multi-view re-rendering. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of VGGT-CAD for visual CAD reconstruction under different observation configurations.

</details>


### [12] [Multiclass Semantic Segmentation of Wildland Fire Images Using Context-Aware Centralized Copy-Paste Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21241)
*Joon Tai Kim,Nishanth Kunchala,Vishv Patel,Tianle Chen,Ziyu Dong,Daniel Ospina Acero,Roger Williams,Mrinal Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文感知的CCPDA数据增强策略，用于小样本多类野火分割，提升分割性能与场景真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火图像分割的精确标注成本高且劳动密集，动态火灾场景导致带标签数据集稀缺。此前的CCPDA方法通过随机粘贴火焰簇生成人工样本，但随机放置会产生不合理场景（如火焰燃烧在沥青上）。

Method: 提出上下文感知策略：将火焰放置限制在语义有效的目标区域，并选择灰烬-植被组成与源上下文最匹配的位置。该方法保留目标图像中已有火焰区域，防止不合理放置，生成接近真实野火场景的图像。通过数值分析和与其他增强方法的对比，采用加权和的多目标优化（MOO）进行评估。

Result: 上下文感知数据增强策略在分割性能和上下文真实性方面均优于其他增强方法。

Conclusion: 上下文感知的CCPDA能有效提升小样本多类野火数据集的质量与真实性，改善分割表现。

Abstract: Producing accurate annotations for deep learning based image segmentation is both costly and labor intensive. This challenge is especially evident in wildland fire applications, where accurately labeled datasets are scarce due to the difficulty of collecting and annotating dynamic fire scenes. To address this problem, our previous work introduced the Centralized Copy-Paste Data Augmentation (CCPDA) method for semantic segmentation of wildland fire imagery, which generates artificial training samples by randomly pasting fire clusters from source images onto target images. However, random placement can produce contextually unrealistic scenes, such as fire burning on asphalt. In this paper, we present a context-aware strategy designed specifically to improve data quality and realism in small multiclass wildland fire datasets, ensuring that augmented samples remain contextually meaningful. The proposed method restricts fire placement to semantically valid target regions and selects the location whose Ash-Vegetation composition most closely matches the source context. This approach preserves existing fire regions in the target image, prevents unrealistic placements, and maintains contextual accuracy by generating images that resemble real wildland fire scenes. We evaluate the Context-Aware CCPDA strategy through numerical analysis and comparisons with other augmentation methods by a weighted sum-based multi-objective optimization (MOO) approach. The results confirm that the context-aware data augmentation strategy leads to improved segmentation performance and contextual realism, outperforming other augmentation procedures.

</details>


### [13] [SafeStyle: Calibrated Style Residual Injection for Controllable Style-Leakage Trade-off in Diffusion Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21242)
*Zhangping Yang,Min Li,Song Yan,Rong Gao,Xinliang Bi,Guanye Xiong,Yujie He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架SafeStyle，通过对风格残差进行子空间校准与预算约束注入，在保持文本对齐的同时实现风格迁移并抑制参考内容泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考图引导的风格迁移存在风格保真与内容泄漏的权衡：强化条件提升风格但泄漏内容，抑制泄漏又损失风格表达；且纹理主导与几何主导风格的空间组织差异加大了难度。

Method: 免训练框架SafeStyle：从紧凑校准集估计"风格支持"与"内容相关"子空间，保留有用重叠并抑制无用内容变化；随后在自适应空间粒度上迁移纯化后的风格证据，并用显式残差范数预算约束其影响。

Result: 在纹理与几何主导风格上，DINO风格相似度达0.432并保持有竞争力的文本对齐；在语义不相交的泄漏压力基准上，DINO风格相似度0.474，语义泄漏仅0.8%。

Conclusion: SafeStyle在冻结扩散模型中有效平衡了风格保真与参考内容抑制，验证了子空间校准与残差预算注入的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Reference-guided diffusion stylization aims to transfer visual style from a reference image while preserving the semantics specified by a text prompt. However, image conditioning often entangles transferable style cues with reference-specific content, leading to an inherent trade-off: stronger conditioning improves style fidelity but increases content leakage, whereas aggressive suppression reduces leakage at the cost of style expression. This challenge is further complicated by the distinct spatial organization of texture- and geometry-dominant styles. To address these issues, we propose SafeStyle, a training-free framework for calibrated style residual injection in frozen diffusion models. SafeStyle first estimates style-supported and content-associated subspaces from compact calibration sets, preserving their informative overlap while suppressing useless content variations. It then transports the purified style evidence over adaptive spatial granularity and constrains its effective influence through an explicit residual-norm budget. Experiments across texture- and geometry-dominant styles show that SafeStyle achieves a DINO style similarity of 0.432 while maintaining competitive text alignment. On a semantically disjoint leakage-stress benchmark, it further achieves a DINO style similarity of 0.474 with only 0.8\% semantic leakage, demonstrating an effective balance between style fidelity and reference-content suppression.

</details>


### [14] [Geometry-Aware Diffusion Guidance via Curvature-Adaptive Tubular Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21251)
*Enze Jiang,Jinwei He,Zheng Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出曲率自适应管状校正（CAT），一种免训练插件，在噪声相关的几何预算内同时调节法向与切向引导位移，稳定扩散引导采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引导会使扩散采样轨迹进入学习分数支撑不足的区域。现有切向投影方法仅限制一阶偏离等密度面，丢弃了可能有用的法向运动，并忽视弯曲表面上切向运动带来的二阶偏离。

Method: CAT将引导梯度分解为法向与切向分量，法向位移按一阶计费、切向位移按方向曲率计费，通过一维对偶方程求二者联合最优幅度；用Armijo回溯校准有限步长与真实引导目标；用无矩阵方向导数避免构造完整分数雅可比。

Result: 在FFHQ和ImageNet的七个逆问题上提升像素与潜空间宿主采样器，感知指标增益尤其稳定；改进InverseBench的黑洞重建；在每个测试的无分类器引导尺度下取得最低FID，并保持饱和与对比度稳定。

Conclusion: 曲率感知的管状控制可作为稳定扩散引导的可复用机制；并提供管状近似的局部保证、校正的唯一性及目标充分下降的理论结果。

Abstract: Gradient-guided diffusion samplers provide flexible priors for inverse problems and conditional generation, but strong guidance can move the sampling trajectory into regions where the learned score is poorly supported. Existing tangent-projection strategies limit first-order departure from an iso-density surface, yet discard potentially useful normal motion and overlook the second-order departure induced by tangent motion on a curved surface. We introduce curvature-adaptive tubular correction (CAT), a training-free plugin that regulates both effects within a shared, noise-dependent geometric budget. CAT decomposes the guidance gradient into normal and tangent components, charges normal displacement at first order and tangent displacement according to directional curvature, and obtains their jointly optimal magnitudes from a one-dimensional dual equation. Armijo backtracking calibrates the resulting finite step against the actual guidance objective, while matrix-free directional derivatives avoid constructing the full score Jacobian. We establish local guarantees for the tubular approximation, uniqueness of the correction, and sufficient objective decrease. Across seven inverse problems on FFHQ and ImageNet, CAT improves the evaluated pixel- and latent-space host samplers, with particularly consistent gains in perceptual metrics. It also improves black hole reconstruction on InverseBench and yields the lowest FID among the compared methods at every tested classifier-free guidance scale, while maintaining stable saturation and contrast. These results support curvature-aware tubular control as a reusable mechanism for stabilizing diffusion guidance.

</details>


### [15] [Edit-VAR: Taming Visual Autoregressive Model for Precise Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21268)
*Chongbo Zhao,Jiangming Wang,Xilai Wang,Xinyu Wang,Jingyi Tang,Chunjie Hao,Pengjie Song,Yue Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Edit-VAR，首个基于预训练视觉自回归视频模型的免训练、免反演文本引导视频编辑框架，通过概率引导的条件token替换、注意力引导调制、尺度解耦生成和残差引导token剪枝，在编辑保真度、源内容保持、时序一致性和推理效率上整体优于现有免训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导视频编辑需在修改目标内容的同时保持未编辑区域的外观与时序一致性。现有方法存在问题：基于训练的方法需要大量数据和算力；免训练的免反演方法源保持引导过强，限制编辑强度、语义修改不完整；基于反演的方法在潜轨迹恢复中会累积近似误差，导致源内容漂移和时序不一致。因此需要一个既免训练又免反演、且能平衡编辑强度与源保持的框架。

Method: Edit-VAR直接编码源视频为多尺度离散token，进行概率引导的条件token替换以保持源内容；采用注意力引导的token级与尺度感知调制，在编辑相关位置和生成阶段选择性地放松源约束；通过尺度解耦生成（即晚期尺度约束释放）重建运动一致细节、减少纹理破碎；并用残差引导token剪枝，利用最后两个高分辨率尺度的冗余降低推理成本。

Result: 大量实验和盲测用户研究表明，Edit-VAR在编辑保真度、源内容保持、时序一致性和推理效率方面整体优于现有免训练视频编辑方法。

Conclusion: Edit-VAR验证了免训练、免反演范式结合视觉自回归视频模型进行文本引导视频编辑的可行性与优越性，为兼顾编辑强度、源保持与效率的视频编辑提供了新思路。

Abstract: Text-guided video editing modifies target content while preserving the appearance and temporal coherence of unedited regions. Training-based approaches provide strong control but demand substantial data and computation. Training-free methods fall into inversion-free and inversion-based paradigms. Inversion-free approaches avoid trajectory recovery, but their source-preserving guidance can limit editing strength and leave semantic changes incomplete. Inversion-based approaches recover a latent trajectory before regeneration, where approximation errors can accumulate and cause source-content drift and temporal inconsistency. We introduce Edit-VAR, the first training-free and inversion-free framework for text-guided video editing with a pretrained visual autoregressive video model. Edit-VAR directly encodes the source video into multi-scale discrete tokens and performs probability-guided conditional token replacement for source preservation. Attention-guided token-wise and scale-aware modulation selectively relaxes source constraints over edit-relevant positions and generation stages. Scale-Decoupled Generation, implemented as late-scale constraint release, regenerates motion-consistent details and reduces texture fragmentation. Residual-guided token pruning further exploits redundancy at the final two high-resolution scales to reduce inference cost. Extensive experiments and a blind user study demonstrate that Edit-VAR outperforms existing training-free video editing methods overall in editing fidelity, source preservation, temporal coherence, and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [16] [Beyond Exact Match: Task-Aware GRPO for Cross-Domain PCBA Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21276)
*Jia Li,Li Dai,Peng Jia,Zhenzhen Hu,Chee Seng Chan,Bingkun Bao,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨域PCBA视觉问答的多模态推理框架，通过统一指令格式、验证推理轨迹与任务感知GRPO奖励设计，在官方挑战赛排行榜取得83.24综合得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动PCBA检测需要联合推理细粒度视觉线索、元件语义与制造知识。尽管大型视觉语言模型提供基础，但标准衍生样本与真实产线图像之间存在域偏移，且任务输出空间异构（选择型与计数型），导致部署受阻。

Method: 将标准衍生、真实世界及辅助PCB域数据转为统一指令格式，构建与视觉证据、问题语义、候选选项和真值答案对齐的验证推理轨迹；提出任务感知GRPO，融合选择型题的多组件语义奖励、计数型题的距离感知奖励及格式奖励；推理阶段结合答案-选项语义一致性校正、自一致性投票与多模型仲裁。

Result: 在官方PCBA Standard-to-Real Grand Challenge排行榜上取得83.24的综合得分。

Conclusion: 任务感知奖励设计与稳健推理策略对跨域PCBA视觉问答有效，验证了该框架的可行性。

Abstract: In automated Printed Circuit Board Assembly (PCBA) inspection, standards-guided decisions require systems to jointly reason over fine-grained visual cues, component semantics, and manufacturing knowledge. Although large vision-language models (VLMs) provide a promising foundation, their deployment is hindered by the domain shift between standards-derived samples and real-world production-line imagery, together with heterogeneous output spaces spanning choice-based and numerical counting tasks. To address these challenges, we propose a multimodal reasoning framework for cross-domain PCBA visual question answering. The framework converts standards-derived, real-world, and auxiliary PCB-domain data into a unified instruction format and constructs verified reasoning traces aligned with visual evidence, question semantics, candidate options, and ground-truth answers. We further introduce Task-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), which moves beyond exact-match supervision by integrating multi-component semantic rewards for choice-based questions, distance-aware rewards for counting questions, and an auxiliary format reward for valid outputs. During inference, answer-option semantic consistency correction, self-consistency voting, and multi-model arbitration are combined to improve prediction robustness. The proposed system achieves an Overall Score of 83.24 on the official PCBA Standard-to-Real Grand Challenge leaderboard, demonstrating the effectiveness of task-aware reward design and robust inference for cross-domain PCBA visual question answering.

</details>


### [17] [Combining Object Detection with Geometry-Aware Clustering to Distinguish Overlapping Plants in UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21304)
*Ik Jae Lee,Hieu D. Nguyen,Mahbubur Meenar,Carlos Morrison Martinez,Cameron Connelly*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何感知的检测后处理框架，利用RGB无人机影像中的部件质心与径向交点（RIP）进行聚类，解决密集冠层中植株重叠被误检为单一对象的问题，从而提升植株级测量的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机影像的植株级信息对自动化作物监测很重要，但在密集冠层中相邻植株常重叠、被检测为单个对象，导致植株级测量可靠性下降。现有方案往往依赖深度传感器、像素级分割或三维重建，成本高且需重新训练检测器。

Method: 框架将目标检测与植物部件的几何聚类结合：对每个灌木级区域内的叶片/枝条，提取两类互补几何特征——部件质心与由检测结构推导的径向交点（RIP）。用K-means和高斯混合模型判断该区域包含单株还是两株重叠植株；通过密度滤波抑制虚假径向交点；再用后处理集成融合空间与方向几何信息。无需深度传感器、像素级分割、三维重建或重训主检测器。

Result: 在田间茄子与番茄无人机影像上评估：基于质心的聚类在茄子上F1为0.89；质心-RIP组合方法在番茄上表现最佳，K-means下准确率0.80、精确率1.00、F1为0.75；密度滤波显著改善了番茄的RIP聚类效果。

Conclusion: 该方案是一种轻量、模块化的工程解法，可接入现有RGB无人机监测流程。结果表明，对现有检测器输出施加几何推理，能补充深度学习目标检测，改善密集农业冠层中的植株级解译。

Abstract: Reliable plant-level information from unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) imagery is important for automated crop monitoring. However, in dense crop canopies, adjacent plants frequently overlap and are detected as a single object, reducing the reliability of plant-level measurements. This study presents a geometry-aware post-detection framework for resolving overlapping plant instances using standard RGB UAV imagery.
  The framework combines object detection with geometric clustering of plant components. Leaves or branches detected within each bush-level region are represented using two complementary geometric features: component centroids and radial intersection points (RIPs) derived from detected plant structures. K-means and Gaussian mixture models determine whether a detected region contains a single plant or two overlapping plants. Density filtering suppresses spurious radial intersections, and a post-pipeline ensemble combines spatial and directional geometric information.
  The framework was evaluated using UAV imagery of eggplant and tomato crops under field conditions. Centroid-based clustering achieved an F1-score of 0.89 for eggplant, while the combined centroid-RIP approach achieved the best tomato performance, with an accuracy of 0.80, precision of 1.00, and F1-score of 0.75 using K-means. Density filtering substantially improved RIP-based clustering for tomato.
  The proposed approach provides a lightweight, modular engineering solution that can be integrated with existing RGB UAV monitoring pipelines without additional depth sensors, pixel-level segmentation, three-dimensional reconstruction, or retraining of the primary bush detector. The results demonstrate that geometric reasoning applied to existing detector outputs can complement deep-learning-based object detection and improve plant-level interpretation in dense agricultural canopies.

</details>


### [18] [S3VD: Semantic-Guidance Spatio-Temporal Scanning for Video Deraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21322)
*Kui Jiang,Yiang Chen,Yan Luo,Zhaocheng Yu,Junjun Jiang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S3VD: 面向视频去雨的语义引导时空扫描框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强降雨会破坏视频高频细节并引入运动模糊，严重影响视觉任务可靠性。现有Mamba等状态空间模型虽具线性复杂度与长程依赖建模优势，但在雨天视频中难以保持2D空间语义完整性及建模3D时空关联。

Method: 提出S3VD框架，含两个模块：多尺度语义融合模块（MSSF）引入DINOv2时序语义先验，弥补Mamba一维展平导致的局部语义损失；时空扫描融合模块（STSF）设计解耦门控Mamba层（DG-Mamba），用两个独立门控自适应控制输入片段的前后上下文，优化帧内与帧间关联建模。

Result: 在视频去雨基准上取得最优性能，相比Mamba基线平均PSNR提升0.84 dB。

Conclusion: S3VD通过语义引导的时空扫描有效突破了Mamba在视频去雨中的局限，验证了语义先验与解耦门控扫描机制的有效性。

Abstract: Heavy rainfall severely degrades outdoor videos by corrupting high-frequency details and introducing motion blur, critically undermining the reliability of visual tasks. Recently, State Space Models (SSMs), particularly Mamba, have emerged as efficient alternatives for vision tasks with their linear complexity and ability to model long-range dependencies. However, when confronted with the poor visual representations in rainy videos, Mamba still faces difficulties in preserving the integrity of 2D spatial semantics and modeling 3D spatio-temporal correlations. To break these limitations, we introduce S3VD, a Semantic-Guidance Spatio-Temporal Scanning framework for video deraining, featuring two key innovations: Multi-Scale Semantic Fusion (MSSF) Module and Spatio-Temporal Scanning Fusion (STSF) Module. The former integrates temporal semantic priors from DINOv2 to guide precise feature representation and counteract the loss of local semantic context inherent to Mamba's 1D flatten operation, enhancing robustness against extreme degradation. The latter introduces a spatio-temporal scanning mechanism and devises a Decoupled-Gating Mamba (DG-Mamba) layer, which employs two independent gates to adaptively control preceding and subsequent contextual information within the input clip, optimizing intra-frame and inter-frame correlation modeling. Experiments on video deraining benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of S3VD, achieving state-of-the-art performance with an average 0.84 dB PSNR improvement over Mamba-based baselines.

</details>


### [19] [VeriFuse: Bounded Vision-Language Arbitration and Reason-Guided Refinement for Cooperative 3D Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21323)
*Hongyi Lin,Yiyao Liu,Qi Kang,Heye Huang,Yang Liu,Haris Koutsopoulos,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VeriFuse 框架，让冻结的 VLM 在受限仲裁角色中为车路协同 3D 检测选择、精修或拒绝候选框，在 DAIR-V2X 上取得 0.494/0.357 的协同 3D AP50/AP70，并将 300 ms 延迟下车辆侧 BEV AP50 相对下降限制在 1.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）具备较强的场景理解与语义判断能力，但其在协同感知中的合适角色尚不清晰：直接让 VLM 回归 3D 检测不可靠且计算昂贵；而仅用它选择单一来源的输出又会丢弃其他智能体的有用信息。因此需要为 VLM 设计一个明确且受限的职责边界。

Method: VeriFuse 是一个面向车路协同 3D 检测的有界仲裁框架。每个智能体先独立生成检测结果；随后围绕车辆侧与路侧提案，构建源条件化的几何候选，并将原始检测、其扰动以及跨源假设合并为统一候选池。冻结的 VLM 在此候选池上执行三种可允许动作：SELECT（选择合适候选）、REFINE（当目标存在但所有候选几何上均不合适时，对现有锚框进行精修）、REJECT（拒绝无支撑的路侧独有提案）。

Result: 在 DAIR-V2X 数据集上，VeriFuse 达到 0.494/0.357 的协同 3D AP50/AP70；在 300 ms 延迟下，车辆侧 BEV AP50 的相对下降被限制在 1.7%。

Conclusion: VeriFuse 为 VLM 在协同感知中分配了清晰且有约束的角色：由语义推理解决跨智能体假设之间的歧义，而由确定性约束决定最终 3D 几何。该方法在保持几何可靠性（延迟鲁棒性）的同时发挥了 VLM 的语义判断优势。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong scene understanding and semantic judgment across diverse tasks, but their appropriate role in cooperative perception remains unclear. Directly asking a VLM to regress 3D detections is unreliable and computationally expensive, whereas using it to select the output of a single source discards useful information from other agents. We introduce VeriFuse, a bounded arbitration framework for vehicle-infrastructure cooperative 3D detection. Each agent first produces detections independently. Around each vehicle and roadside proposal, VeriFuse generates source-conditioned geometric candidates and combines the original detections, their perturbations, and cross-source hypotheses into a unified candidate pool. A frozen VLM then chooses among three admissible actions: SELECT an adequate candidate; REFINE an existing anchor when an object is supported but all candidates are geometrically inadequate; or REJECT an unsupported infrastructure-only proposal. Experiments on the DAIR-V2X dataset show that VeriFuse achieves 0.494/0.357 cooperative 3D AP50/AP70 and limits the relative vehicle-side BEV AP50 drop under a 300 ms delay to 1.7%. Overall, VeriFuse assigns the VLM a clear and constrained role in cooperative perception: semantic reasoning resolves ambiguity among cross-agent hypotheses, while deterministic constraints determine the final 3D geometry.

</details>


### [20] [Cube-Splat: High-Fidelity 360° Gaussian Splatting SLAM via Cubemap Factorization and Adjoint-Consistent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21347)
*Xiangfei Guo,Hao Shi,Yufan Zhang,Zhonghua Yi,Yongqi Mao,Xiaoting Yin,Kaiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Cube-Splat是首个全景3D高斯泼溅SLAM框架，将360°图像分解为四张共享光心的立方体贴图虚拟针孔视图，实现统一位姿状态的一致更新，并构建了SynPano数据集，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS视觉SLAM多针对针孔相机设计，难以直接适用于全景（360°）图像，因此需要专门的全景GS-SLAM框架。

Method: 将每个360°帧分解为四张固定朝向、共享单一光心的立方体贴图虚拟针孔视图；以前面为主位姿状态，通过伴随映射（adjoint mapping）汇聚所有面的梯度，使多面观测协同更新单一状态并保持跨视图几何一致性；建图模块利用聚合的立方体贴图光线稠密化并优化各向异性高斯。同时提出可扩展、照片级真实的合成数据集SynPano。

Result: 在PALVIO、OmniBlender两个公开基准及自建SynPano数据集（涵盖室内外场景）上，Cube-Splat在跟踪精度与重建保真度上均达到SOTA。

Conclusion: Cube-Splat验证了立方体贴图表征在全景GS-SLAM中的有效性，并提供开源代码与SynPano数据集以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Recent progress in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has enabled dense visual SLAM with pinhole cameras, yet most pipelines are not designed for panoramic imagery. We present Cube-Splat, the first panoramic GS-SLAM framework that factorizes each 360° frame into a cubemap of four fixed-orientation virtual pinhole views sharing a single optical center. By designating the front face as the primary pose state, we accumulate gradients from all faces via an adjoint mapping, thereby enabling multi-face observations to coherently update a single state while strictly preserving cross-view geometric consistency. Concurrently, our mapping module densifies and optimizes anisotropic Gaussians using aggregated cubemap rays for high-fidelity, dense reconstruction. Furthermore, to rigorously evaluate panoramic SLAM under diverse and challenging conditions, we introduce SynPano, a highly scalable, photorealistic synthetic dataset featuring parameterized complex trajectories and multi-modal ground truth. Extensive evaluations on two public benchmarks (PALVIO and OmniBlender) and our SynPano dataset, collectively encompassing both indoor and outdoor scenes, demonstrate that Cube-Splat achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in tracking accuracy and reconstruction fidelity. Both the source code and the SynPano dataset are available at https://github.com/guoxf304/CubeSplat.

</details>


### [21] [PrismAlign: Prior-Steered Multi-View VLM Alignment for Hallucination-Robust Table OCR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21351)
*Guangyi Liu,Qianjun Huang,Boyu Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PrismAlign多VLM框架，通过对齐多样视觉视角并引入表格逻辑先验和贝叶斯决策策略，减少表格抽取中的结构错误与语义幻觉，在多个基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格抽取常出现结构性错误与语义幻觉，单一视觉语言模型输出存在歧义，需要更可靠的抽取方法。

Method: 构建多VLM框架PrismAlign，融合多重视觉视角以消解歧义；引入表格逻辑先验评估输出合理性，将结构对齐与单元格内容对齐解耦；利用抽取错误与可计算规则违反之间的相关性，设计贝叶斯决策策略最大化对齐准确率。

Result: 在开源与自研VLM上评测，PrismAlign减少了幻觉，并在OmniDocBench 1.5以及CC-OCR、PureDocBench的表格类别上达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 多视角对齐结合逻辑先验与贝叶斯决策能有效提升表格抽取的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Table extraction suffers from frequent structural errors and semantic hallucinations. We propose PrismAlign, a multi-VLM framework aligning diverse visual perspectives to resolve ambiguity. It integrates priors of table logic to assess output plausibility, decoupling structural alignment from cell content alignment. A Bayesian decision strategy maximizes alignment accuracy by exploiting the correlation between extraction errors and computable rule violations. Evaluated on open-source and custom VLMs, PrismAlign reduces hallucinations and achieves state-of-the-art performance on OmniDocBench 1.5, as well as on the table category of CC-OCR and PureDocBench.

</details>


### [22] [Field Tracking of Insects Using a Stereoscopic Event-Based Camera Setup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21354)
*Pratham G. Shenwai,Martin J. Lankheet,John T. Hrynuk,Mandiyam Y. Mahadeeswara,Mandyam V. Srinivasan,Sridhar Ravi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出将异步事件相机数据转换为传统视频格式，并结合立体配置，实现野外快速移动小生物的低延迟三维追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速追踪自然环境中的小型快速移动生物对理解其行为与生态意义重大，但传统帧式成像受低时间分辨率导致运动模糊及数据存储限制，亟需更自适应的成像方案。

Method: 采用事件相机（记录像素级亮度变化而非整帧）提升时间分辨率与数据效率；将异步事件转换为传统视频格式以复用成熟图像分析方法，并与立体（双目）配置耦合。

Result: 实现了在野外条件下对快速移动目标的连续、低延迟三维追踪，显著减轻运动伪影，更准确地表征动物运动。

Conclusion: 该方法使事件成像更易应用于自然野外场景，可支撑动物行为与生态研究、农业管理及其他需要野外高保真目标追踪的领域。

Abstract: High-speed tracking of small, fast-moving organisms in their natural environments is important to better understand their behavior and ecology. Traditional frame-based imaging suffers from motion blur due to low temporal resolution, and data storage limitations, propelling a search for more adaptive solutions. Event cameras, which capture changes in brightness at pixel level instead of entire frames, have emerged as a promising solution by increasing temporal resolution and data efficiency. Here, we demonstrate the use of event-based imaging with standard video-based processing methods by converting the asynchronous events into conventional video formats, allowing us to leverage the event camera's enhanced temporal detail to capture intricate insect flight movements and apply established image analysis techniques. Coupling this conversion process with a stereoscopic configuration provides continuous, low-latency, three-dimensional tracking of fast-moving subjects in field conditions. As a result, we substantially mitigate motion artifacts and achieve more accurate representations of animal movements. By making event-based imaging more readily applicable in natural field settings, our method support broader applications across animal behavior and ecological research, agricultural management, and other fields requiring high-fidelity object tracking in the wild.

</details>


### [23] [Hiding in Plain Sight: A Diffusion-based Mitigation of Geolocation Privacy Leakage in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21363)
*Yining Wang,Xi Li,Mi Zhang,Xiaohan Zhang,Xiaoyu You,Zhenxing Qian,Mi Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多模态大推理模型（MLRM）驱动的地理位置隐私泄露问题，并提出一种基于扩散模型的主动防御框架，在扩散逆向采样的隐空间中注入扰动，借助GeoCLIP作为代理模型精准破坏地理信号，实现强黑盒迁移性并保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLRM能从随意分享的照片中通过结构化推理（建筑风格、植被、光照等线索）精准推断用户地理位置，构成严重的隐私威胁；而现有的拒绝式防护易被越狱提示绕过（响应率可达100%），像素空间扰动防御又存在黑盒迁移性差和视觉伪影等结构性缺陷，因此亟需更强的主动防御方案。

Method: 提出基于扩散模型的防御框架：在扩散模型逆向采样的隐空间中注入扰动，直接作用于高层语义表示；并以与GPS坐标显式对齐的GeoCLIP模型作为代理，定位并破坏MLRM用于位置推断的地理信号。

Result: 精心构造的越狱提示可将模型响应率提升至100%；现有像素空间防御黑盒迁移性下降且视觉伪影明显；所提方法在保持感知图像质量的同时，显著提升了黑盒迁移性。

Conclusion: 隐空间语义扰动从构造上解决了效果—可用性的瓶颈，能提供针对地理位置隐私泄露的定向、主动防御，并可无缝集成到社交媒体平台。

Abstract: Multimodal large reasoning models (MLRMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in complex visual understanding. However, this very power introduces a critical yet underexplored privacy threat: adversaries can exploit MLRMs to precisely infer users' geographic locations from casually shared photographs, by performing structured reasoning over subtle visual cues such as architectural styles, vegetation, and lighting conditions. In this work, we present a systematic study of MLRM-driven geolocation privacy leakage. We first reveal that refusal-based safeguards are critically insufficient, as carefully crafted jailbreak prompts can raise model response rates to 100%. We further identify that existing defenses, which inject imperceptible perturbations into shared images, suffer from structural limitations intrinsic to their pixel-space optimization, resulting in degraded black-box transferability and pronounced visual artifacts. Motivated by these findings, we propose a diffusion-based framework that provides targeted, proactive defense against geolocation privacy leakage. By injecting perturbations into the latent space of a diffusion model during reverse sampling, our method operates directly on high-level semantic representations, thereby resolving the effectiveness-utility bottlenecks by construction. We further ground our optimization with GeoCLIP, a model explicitly aligned with GPS coordinates, as a surrogate to pinpoint and disrupt the geographic signals that MLRMs exploit for location inference. This targeted semantic disruption yields significantly stronger black-box transferability while preserving perceptual image quality, offering a seamless integration on social media platforms.

</details>


### [24] [RobotEQ-Video: A Video-Centric Benchmark for Social Proactive Intelligence with World-State Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21371)
*Xinyi Che,Zheng Lian,Kuofei Fang,Xuehao Wang,Xinghai Gao,Junqing Wu,Chuyu Wu,Liyi Liu,Yanhan Huang,Keyi Xie,Haomin Ouyang,Jinyang Wu,Fan Zhang,Runhao Zeng,Xun Yang,Bin He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RobotEQ-Video，将社交主动智能（SPI）研究从静态图像扩展到动态视频，并构建层级化世界状态分类体系以保证场景覆盖的全面性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SPI 研究存在两大局限：一是仅关注静态图像，忽略了动态视频中推断人类状态与需求的关键线索；二是依赖自由形式的数据采集流程，无法保证多样场景的全面覆盖。

Method: 提出 RobotEQ-Video，从图像中心转向视频中心分析；构建四级由粗到细的层级化世界状态分类体系，包含 6 个领域、20 个维度、142 个一级属性和 816 个二级属性，并据此构建基准数据集。

Result: 构建的基准包含 2000+ 视频、100K+ 人工标注和 16K+ 行为恰当性标签。评测显示当前系统仍不可靠，且低于人类表现。此外还探索了世界模型在该任务上的作用。

Conclusion: 该工作将 SPI 研究从静态图像推进到动态视频，并确保基准测试中场景覆盖更加全面。

Abstract: Social Proactive Intelligence (SPI) extends proactive assistance beyond task completeness to consider social appropriateness in diverse embodied scenarios. However, prior SPI research faces two key limitations. First, existing work focuses on static images, whereas dynamic videos provide crucial cues for inferring human states and needs, offering richer information than isolated images. Second, prior work often relies on free-form data collection pipelines, which fail to guarantee comprehensive coverage of diverse scenarios. To address these gaps, we introduce RobotEQ-Video, shifting the focus from image-centric to video-centric analysis. To ensure comprehensive video coverage, we construct a hierarchical world-state taxonomy organized into a four-level coarse-to-fine structure, comprising 6 domains, 20 dimensions, 142 level-1 attributes, and 816 level-2 attributes. The resulting benchmark comprises 2K+ videos with 100K+ human annotations and 16K+ labels for assessing behavior properness. Benchmark evaluation reveals that current systems remain unreliable and fall short of human performance. We further explore how world models can help tackle this task. This work advances SPI research from static images to dynamic videos and ensures more comprehensive scenario coverage during benchmarking.

</details>


### [25] [JEPA Guided Diffusion: Predictive Vision-Language Conditioning for Generative Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21379)
*Trinh Tra Giang Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen Vo,Nguyen Hoai Thuong Bui,Ha Duc Bui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解耦式交通预测框架，冻结V-JEPA编码器提取预测性潜表征，轻量对齐模块将其投影至冻结Cosmos扩散模型的条件空间，仅训练对齐模块即可合成未来视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的交通预测既需理解场景动态，又需合成真实的未来观测。现有基于扩散的视频生成模型虽能产生视觉上合理的预测，但端到端训练代价高昂，且将场景理解与图像合成纠缠在一起，效率与可解释性欠佳。

Method: 将未来表征学习与视频生成解耦：先用冻结的V-JEPA编码器从观测交通视频中提取预测性潜表征，在语义潜空间捕获场景动态；再用轻量潜对齐模块把这些表征投影到冻结Cosmos扩散模块的条件空间，从而无需重新训练大型生成模型即可合成未来视频。所有基础模型均冻结，仅训练轻量对齐模块。

Result: 在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 5基准上取得75.1297分，竞赛排名第三。

Conclusion: V-JEPA学到的预测性世界表征可有效引导下游视频生成，为端到端扩散式预测提供了一种实用且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Accurate traffic forecasting requires both understanding scene dynamics and synthesizing realistic future observations. Recent diffusion-based video generation models produce visually plausible predictions but require expensive end-to-end training and often entangle scene understanding with image synthesis. In this work, we propose a decoupled forecasting framework that separates future representation learning from video generation. A frozen V-JEPA encoder first extracts predictive latent representations from the observed traffic videos, capturing the underlying scene dynamics in a semantic latent space. A lightweight latent alignment module then projects these representations into the conditioning space of a frozen Cosmos diffusion module, enabling future video synthesis without retraining the large generative model. By freezing all foundation models and training only the lightweight alignment module, the proposed framework substantially reduces optimization complexity while preserving forecasting capability. Experimental results on the AI City Challenge 2026 Track 5 benchmark demonstrate that the proposed method achieved a score of 75.1297, ranking third in the competition. These results suggest that predictive world representations learned by V-JEPA can effectively guide downstream video generation, providing a practical and efficient alternative to end-to-end diffusion-based forecasting.

</details>


### [26] [AgentVidBench: A Multi-Hop Video Question Answering Benchmark for Evaluating MLLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21386)
*Seoyeon An,Hyeonseo Jang,Minsu Kim,Chanho Lee,Younghan Park,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentVidBench，一个面向多跳视频问答的基准，用于评估MLLM智能体的空间、时间与因果推理能力，并提供分步解答轨迹以支持轨迹评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型视频理解基准局限于简单场景级查询或全局摘要，仅需单步推理，缺乏能严格评估多跳多模态推理与智能体能力的视频基准。

Method: 构建AgentVidBench多跳视频问答基准，聚焦空间、时间、因果推理；除问答对外，还提供逐步解答轨迹以进行轨迹评估，判断智能体是否明确获取支撑答案所需的证据；并评测12个专有与开源MLLM。

Result: 单轮设置下各模型表现有限；将模型接入先进智能体工作流后，准确率与轨迹得分总体提升；作者还提出一个简单有效的智能体策略作为有竞争力的基线。

Conclusion: AgentVidBench可作为面向智能体视频理解研究的整体性测试平台，推动多跳多模态推理评测发展。

Abstract: Comprehensive video understanding is crucial for advancing artificial intelligence toward the intricate dynamics of the physical world. While recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in video understanding, existing benchmarks remain confined to simple scene-level queries or global summaries that require only single-step inference. Real-world video understanding involves more challenging tasks that require multi-hop multimodal reasoning, and there is a critical absence of video benchmarks equipped to rigorously evaluate these agentic capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce AgentVidBench, a multi-hop video question answering benchmark focused on evaluating the spatial, temporal, and causal reasoning capabilities of MLLM agents. Beyond standard question-answer pairs, AgentVidBench provides step-by-step solution traces to support trajectory evaluation that assesses whether agents explicitly acquire the evidence needed to justify their answers. Experiments with 12 proprietary and open-source MLLMs show that single-turn performance remains limited on AgentVidBench, while integrating these models into state-of-the-art agentic workflows generally improves performance with respect to both accuracy and trajectory scores. We further present a simple yet effective agentic strategy that serves as a competitive baseline on AgentVidBench, establishing our benchmark as a holistic testbed for future research on agentic video understanding. Code and datasets are available at https://github.com/krafton-ai/agentvidbench and https://huggingface.co/datasets/agentvidbench/agentvidbench.

</details>


### [27] [A Scene Language Model for Open-Vocabulary Scene Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21400)
*Adam Lilja,Fabio Hübel,Siming He,Junsheng Fu,Claire Tomlin,Lars Hammarstrand,Jitendra Malik,Jonas Frey,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneLM 提出用单一视觉-语言模型直接维护文本形式的场景地图，以结构化文本对象列表作为唯一持久记忆，实现更紧凑的开放词汇3D场景映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D场景映射系统依赖手工设计的映射流程来关联观测、跨视角融合信息并维持一致性，同时常存储嵌入或图像裁剪等特征丰富的物体表示，导致持久记忆体积大、复杂度高。

Method: 将整个场景表示为结构化文本对象列表，作为模型唯一持久记忆；对每张输入图像，模型读取当前场景状态并通过添加、编辑、删除物体来更新地图。引入迭代式场景地图维护的监督任务，以及无需人工标注、从图像自动生成训练数据的标注流程。

Result: 在语言引导检索基准和定位基准上，SceneLM 生成的场景地图性能可与由专用感知和几何模块构成的完整映射系统竞争，同时场景表示紧凑 6-12 倍。四足机器人实验表明其可在边缘设备上在线运行。

Conclusion: 单一视觉-语言模型仅使用轻量文本表示即可直接维护持久的开放词汇3D场景地图，代码已开源。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D scene mapping aims to build a persistent representation of the objects in an environment. Existing systems typically rely on engineered mapping pipelines to associate observations, merge information across views, and maintain a consistent scene representation over time. Many additionally store feature-rich object representations, such as embeddings or image crops, increasing the size and complexity of the persistent memory. We introduce SceneLM, a Scene-Language Model that directly maintains a textual scene map. The full scene is represented as a structured text list of objects, which serves as the model's only persistent memory. For each input image, the model reads the current scene state and updates the map by adding, editing, and removing objects. To learn this behavior, we introduce supervision tasks for iterative scene map maintenance together with an automatic annotation pipeline that generates training data from images without human labels. We evaluate SceneLM on both a language-grounded retrieval benchmark and a localization benchmark. Across both benchmarks, the model produces a scene map that achieves competitive performance with complete mapping systems built from dedicated perception and geometric modules while producing a scene representation that is 6-12x more compact. We further show that SceneLM can be run online on an edge device through experiments on a quadruped. These results show that a persistent open-vocabulary 3D scene map can be maintained directly by a single vision-language model using only a lightweight text representation. Training and inference code is available on https://goldengait.github.io/scenelm/.

</details>


### [28] [SIRA: Reasoning-Aware Surgical Instrument Segmentation via Query-Anchored Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21402)
*Zhibo Zhang,Qijie Wang,Zengqiang Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了推理感知手术器械分割（RA-SIS）任务与SurgRS数据集，并设计多模态框架SIRA，通过查询锚定双重对齐在像素级实现语义接地，优于现有推理感知基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术器械分割方法多将分割视为类别驱动的定位问题，难以捕捉手术流程中的过程上下文与任务相关语义。

Method: 提出RA-SIS任务，将分割定义为手术上下文下的查询条件推理；构建SurgRS数据集（41,000图文对，实例级掩码与结构化问答监督对齐）；提出SIRA多模态框架，解耦目标级与查询级语义，通过查询锚定双重对齐融合视觉特征，并对齐查询语义与空间特征及分割提示。

Result: 在SurgRS上的大量实验表明，SIRA相较现有推理感知基线取得提升。

Conclusion: RA-SIS任务形式与SIRA框架有效增强了掩码预测中的语义-视觉一致性，推动了手术器械分割向推理感知方向发展。

Abstract: Surgical instrument segmentation (SIS) plays a critical role in robotic assistance and surgical workflow analysis. However, most existing SIS methods formulate segmentation as a category-driven localization problem, limiting their ability to capture procedural context and task-dependent semantics in surgical workflows. We introduce Reasoning-Aware Surgical Instrument Segmentation (RA-SIS), a task formulation that frames segmentation as query-conditioned inference under surgical context. To benchmark this setting, we construct SurgRS, a surgical reasoning segmentation dataset consisting of 41,000 image-text pairs, which aligns instance-level masks with structured query-answer supervision to enable semantic grounding at the pixel level. Based on SurgRS, we propose Surgical Instrument Reasoning and Segmentation Assistant (SIRA), a multimodal framework that disentangles target-level and query-level semantics and integrates them with visual features through query-anchored dual alignment. By aligning query semantics with spatial features and segmentation prompts, SIRA enhances semantic-visual consistency in mask prediction. Extensive experiments on SurgRS demonstrate improvements over existing reasoning-aware baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/linxir226/SIRA.

</details>


### [29] [Quantization-Aware Kalman Estimation for Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21407)
*Qitan Shi,Cheng Jin,Jiawei Zhang,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QuAKE，将量化扩散模型采样中的输出校正建模为在线估计问题，用卡尔曼滤波结合轨迹历史恢复全精度输出，轻量即插即用，在W4A4量化文本到图像扩散模型上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化能降低扩散模型的内存与计算开销，但激进压缩使量化输出严重偏离全精度结果。现有采样阶段校正方法主要依赖局部信息，未能充分利用轨迹历史，因而难以纠正在时间步间传播的误差。

Method: 将使用量化去噪器的采样过程形式化为在线估计问题，利用量化去噪器输出的历史来恢复采样器所需的全精度输出。提出QuAKE（量化感知卡尔曼估计器），结合平滑轨迹先验与条件高斯观测模型。每个采样步以闭式递归更新输出窗口的后验，并将后验均值输入采样器。该方法为轻量即插即用校正器，无需修改量化网络，并天然支持任意高阶多步ODE采样器。

Result: 在W4A4量化的文本到图像扩散模型上，QuAKE在减小与全精度采样的分布差异方面持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: QuAKE通过卡尔曼式在线估计有效利用轨迹历史进行采样阶段校正，是一种通用、轻量且即插即用的量化扩散模型误差校正方案。

Abstract: Quantization offers a practical path to deploying diffusion models with reduced memory and computation, but aggressive compression can cause quantized outputs to deviate substantially from their full-precision counterparts. Sampling-stage correction methods seek to compensate for such deviations during sampling, but existing approaches rely primarily on local information and underexploit trajectory history, limiting their ability to correct errors that propagate across timesteps. In this work, we formulate sampling with a quantized denoiser as an online estimation problem, using the history of quantized denoiser outputs to recover the underlying full-precision outputs required by the sampler. We propose QuAKE, a Quantization-Aware Kalman Estimator that combines a smooth trajectory prior with a conditional Gaussian observation model. At each sampling step, QuAKE recursively updates the posterior over the output window in closed form and feeds its posterior mean to the sampler. QuAKE is a lightweight plug-and-play corrector that requires no modification to the quantized network and naturally supports arbitrary high-order multistep ODE samplers. Experiments across W4A4-quantized text-to-image diffusion models show that QuAKE consistently outperforms existing methods in reducing the distributional discrepancy from full-precision sampling.

</details>


### [30] [When Online Adaptation Hurts: Parameter-Frozen Test-Time Ensembling for Continual Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21412)
*Ruijie Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出参数冻结的推理增强（PIE）方案：利用源训练分割模型对解剖保持的尺度与翻转视图进行预测，映射回原位置并平均概率，在不更新权重与归一化统计的前提下提升跨站点/厂商/协议的医学图像分割稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割器在站点、扫描仪厂商或协议变化时性能显著下降。持续测试时适应（CTTA）虽无需目标标签，但在非平稳数据流上难以更新模型且易引入大量错误，因此需要一种更合理、更有意义的替代方案。

Method: 提出参数冻结推理增强（PIE）：使用源训练的分割器学习解剖保持的尺度变换与翻转视图，将其预测映射回原始位置，并对概率进行平均；全程不修改模型权重，也不改变归一化统计量。同时在M&amp;Ms心脏MRI（厂商A训练，依次在B、C、D评估）上开展对照消融。

Result: PIE取得0.7786平均Dice，高于仅源推理的0.7680，以及五种在线适应基线的0.7388–0.7416。消融显示性能在28个视图时饱和；置信度加权、类别先验校正、连通域滤波、形态学细化与层间平滑无效果或导致负迁移。心脏MRI与眼底图像的定性结果与冻结集成一致，能更好保留较细及嵌套解剖结构。

Conclusion: 结果为医学CTTA提供了稳健强基线，并揭示一个重要失效模式：适应与手工细化可能不如精心设计的推理可靠。

Abstract: Medical image segmenters often get worse when sites, scanner vendors, or protocols change. Continual test-time adaptation (CTTA) addresses this problem without target labels, but it can be impossible to update a model on a non-stationary stream and can lead to a lot of errors. We examine a more reasonable and meaningful alternative: parameter-frozen inference enhancement(PIE). We use a source-trained segmenter that learns about anatomy-preserving scale and flip views, maps their predictions back to the native location, and averages the probabilities. We do not modify the weights of the model or the normalization statistics. On a cardiac MRI stream from M\&Ms, which is trained on vendor A and evaluated sequentially on vendors B, C, and D, PIE has 0.7786 mean Dice, compared to 0.7680 for source-only inference and 0.7388--0.7416 for five other online-adaptation baselines. The controlled ablations show that performance saturates at 28 views, and confidence weighting, class-prior correction, connected-component filtering, morphological refinement, and inter-slice smoothing have no effect or cause negative transfer. Qualitative results on cardiac MRI and fundus images are also consistent with the frozen ensemble keeping thinner and nested anatomical structures. These results provide a strong, stable baseline for medical CTTA and expose an important failure mode: adaptation and handcrafted refinement can be less reliable than carefully designed inference.

</details>


### [31] [P$^3$-SAM: SAM with Perceptual Parallel Prompt for Few-Shot Strip Steel Surface Defect Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21424)
*Qian Xu,Hang Xiong,Anpeng Wang,Sam Kwong,Cong Zhang,Runmin Cong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出P$^3$-SAM框架，通过感知优化编码与并行提示生成器两项策略，将SAM适配于钢材表面缺陷的少样本语义分割，在三个基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钢材表面缺陷少样本语义分割（S$^3$D）与自然场景差异显著，存在局部对比度低、光照不均、细粒度纹理复杂等工业特性，导致直接迁移基于SAM的方法时性能下降。

Method: 提出感知并行提示（P$^3$）框架，构建P$^3$-SAM模型，包含两大策略：感知优化编码（POE）增强局部对比度并保留关键纹理细节；并行提示生成器（PPG）同时生成语义提示与空间提示，为SAM解码器提供全面引导。

Result: 在三个少样本S$^3$D基准上取得最优性能，其中在Surface Defects-4i数据集上mIoU提升达12.00%。

Conclusion: 通过针对工业缺陷特性设计感知优化编码与并行提示生成，可有效激活SAM的预训练表征能力，显著提升钢材表面缺陷少样本分割效果。

Abstract: Few-shot semantic segmentation (FSS) of strip steel surface defects (S$^3$D) has posed significant challenges distinct from natural scenes. Unlike natural images, S$^3$D task exhibits unique characteristics including low local contrast, uneven illumination, and complex fine-grained texture patterns. Although recent methods based on Segment Anything Model (SAM) have shown promise in FSS on natural images by leveraging SAM's powerful pre-trained representations, these unique industrial characteristics of S$^3$D images lead to performance drop when directly applying SAM to industrial defect scenarios. In this paper, we propose a novel Perceptual Parallel Prompt (P$^3$) framework that empowers SAM, creating the P$^3$-SAM model to address these challenges through two core strategies. First, we develop a Perceptual-Optimized Encoding (POE) strategy that enhances local contrast and preserves critical texture details for S$^3$D segmentation. Second, we introduce the Parallel Prompt Generator (PPG) strategy that simultaneously generates both semantic and spatial prompts, enabling comprehensive guidance for SAM's decoder across varying images. Extensive experiments on three few-shot S$^3$D benchmarks demonstrate that P$^3$-SAM achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly notable improvements of 12.00% in mIoU on Surface Defects-4i dataset.

</details>


### [32] [Think Locally, Refine Globally for Memory-Efficient 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21437)
*Jingke Zhou,Chenhang Ma,Zhizhou Zhong,Mingkai Liu,Zhuang Zhou,Yicheng ji,Binghua Su,Bo Cai,Xianliang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LoG-VGGT：一种内存高效的长序列3D重建框架，通过跨窗口注意力与全局相机一致性精炼，在受限内存下兼顾局部时序建模与全局相机一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列3D重建需兼顾局部时序建模与全局相机一致性，但全局注意力内存开销大，难以扩展。如何在内存受限条件下实现跨窗口信息传播并抑制长期位姿漂移是核心问题。

Method: 1）不依赖全局注意力，在少量transformer块中引入跨窗口注意力，实现相邻时序窗口间信息传播且内存有界；2）设计全局相机一致性精炼模块，相机token与紧凑寄存器token通过交叉注意力交互，施加全序列场景级约束，联合优化相机表示。

Result: 在多个长序列基准上取得更优的深度精度与稳健的相机位姿估计，并具备有竞争力的流式重建性能。

Conclusion: LoG-VGGT以受限内存实现了长时位姿稳定性的提升，无需序列级全局注意力的高成本，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: We propose LoG-VGGT, a memory-efficient framework for long-sequence 3D reconstruction that balances local temporal modeling with global camera consistency. Instead of relying on full global attention, our method introduces cross-window attention at a small subset of transformer blocks, enabling effective information propagation across adjacent temporal windows while keeping memory usage bounded. To mitigate long-term pose drift, we further design a global camera consistency refinement module, where camera tokens interact with compact register tokens via cross-attention to enforce scene-level constraints across the entire sequence. This design enables joint optimization of camera representations and significantly improves long-horizon pose stability without incurring the high cost of sequence-wide attention. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LoG-VGGT achieves improved depth accuracy and robust camera pose estimation across multiple long-sequence benchmarks, while delivering competitive streaming reconstruction performance.

</details>


### [33] [ME-Dex 1.0: Bringing Heterogeneous Tactile Sensing into World Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21449)
*Xuancheng Zhang,Xuetao Liu,Qianying Tang,Jizhe Wang,Zhijing Cheng,Bochen Lin,Haoran Wen,Ming Li,Kun Zhan,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ME-Dex-1.0统一世界-动作-触觉模型，将触觉作为未来观测与视频、动作联合建模，提升机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型将视频预测能力引入机器人动作生成，但触觉方法通常仅作为条件输入，未与未来触觉状态、视觉观测和动作联合预测。作者认为触觉信号如同视频一样提供世界状态演化的观测，应被建模为未来观测。

Method: 采用Mixture-of-Transformers架构，包含Video Expert、Tactile Expert和Action Expert，均以flow matching训练，中间层通过共享注意力连接，使动作生成能利用视觉与触觉动态表示。提出Canonical Hand Model与Unified Tactile Autoencoder，将不同本体与传感布局的触觉观测映射到共享空间与潜空间。构建Agentic Tactile Data Engine，通过agent化数据生产平台，为RoboTwin和DexJoCo补充仿真轨迹回放中直接由力传感器记录的触觉数据。

Result: 在RoboTwin、DexJoCo和ManiFeel仿真平台及真实机器人评估中，使用配备触觉传感的夹爪和灵巧手均取得操作性能提升。

Conclusion: 将触觉作为未来观测与视频、动作统一联合建模，能够有效增强世界动作模型的机器人操作能力，并具备跨本体与跨传感布局的泛化潜力。

Abstract: World Action Models bring the predictive capabilities of video models into robot action generation, providing a rich foundation for modeling future visual states. Tactile sensing complements this foundation with direct measurements of physical interaction. Some existing methods use tactile features as conditioning inputs without jointly predicting future tactile states, visual observations, and actions. Our key insight is that tactile signals, like video, provide observations of the evolving world state and should be modeled as future observations alongside video. We present ME-Dex-1.0 (MachEmbodied-Dex-1.0), a unified World Action Tactile Model for joint visual, tactile, and action learning. ME-Dex-1.0 adopts a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture comprising a Video Expert, a Tactile Expert, and an Action Expert, all trained with flow matching. We use shared attention connects the experts in intermediate layers, allowing action generation to draw on learned representations of visual and tactile dynamics during joint denoising. To support multi-source heterogeneous tactile inputs, a Canonical Hand Model and a Unified Tactile Autoencoder map tactile observations from different embodiments and sensing layouts into shared spatial and latent spaces. To address the limited availability of paired visual, tactile, and action data, we develop the Agentic Tactile Data Engine, an agent-based data production platform. It supplements RoboTwin and DexJoCo with tactile data recorded directly from force sensors during trajectory replay in simulation. Experiments on the RoboTwin, DexJoCo, and ManiFeel simulation platforms, together with real robot evaluations, demonstrate improved manipulation performance using both grippers and dexterous hands equipped with tactile sensing.

</details>


### [34] [CompAdapt: Adaptable Composite Motion Modeling for Physics-Consistent Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21455)
*Haoran Qin,Renlong Wu,Tianyu Huang,Yukang Ding,Hui Li,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CompAdapt，一个物理一致的文本到视频框架，支持复合物理行为、语言到物理语义的映射及一次性适配，在物理一致性上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散 T2V 模型常违背基本物理动力学；已有物理约束方法仅限单一类型运动、依赖人工参数，且难以泛化到未见物理规律。

Method: 将神经动力学建模从单一运动扩展到复合物理行为（耦合运动、多阶段过渡、多物体碰撞）；把自然语言提示转化为结构化物理语义，端到端指定运动类型、时间关系与初始物理参数；提出动力学感知先验匹配实现无需重训练核心动力学模块的一次性适配；引入物理感知潜在特征融合模块提升快速复杂运动下的视觉保真度。

Result: 在物理导向的 T2V 基准上，物理一致性优于通用 T2V 模型与物理约束基线，同时保持高视觉质量及对未见动力学的适应性。

Conclusion: CompAdapt 通过复合动力学建模、语言到物理语义转换与先验匹配，实现了复杂真实场景下可适配且物理一致的视频生成。

Abstract: While diffusion-based text-to-video (T2V) models have demonstrated impressive capability in generating realistic and temporally coherent videos, they often fail to respect fundamental physical dynamics. Although recent physics-constrained methods incorporate explicit dynamics priors to improve physical plausibility, they remain limited to simple single-type motions, depend on manually specified parameters, and struggle to generalize to unseen physical laws. In this work, we propose CompAdapt, a physics-consistent T2V framework for adaptable generation across complex real-world scenarios. It extends neural dynamics modeling beyond single-type motions to encompass composite physical behaviors, including coupled motions, multi-stage transitions, and multi-object collisions. Furthermore, CompAdapt translates natural language prompts into structured physical semantics, enabling end-to-end specification of motion types, temporal relations, and initial physical parameters. To generalize to novel physical environments, CompAdapt introduces dynamics-aware prior matching, achieving one-shot adaptation without retraining the core dynamics module. In addition, a physics-aware latent feature fusion module improves visual fidelity under fast and complex motion. Experiments on physics-focused T2V benchmarks demonstrate that CompAdapt improves physical consistency over both general T2V models and physics-constrained baselines, while preserving high visual quality and adaptability to unseen dynamics. The project page is available at https://makapic.github.io/CompAdapt/ .

</details>


### [35] [PSEE: Progressive Sensor Event Expansion for Point-Supervised Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21462)
*Jiaxi Yin,Ge Wang,Han Ding,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究点监督下的可穿戴传感器时间动作定位，提出PSEE方法通过逐步扩展伪片段来恢复边界信息，在四个惯性传感基准上提升了伪边界质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴传感器时间动作定位（TAL）比传统动作识别提供更细粒度的理解，但训练通常需要每个动作实例昂贵的起止时间标注。为降低标注负担，作者研究点监督TAL，即每个实例仅标注一个时间戳和类别。

Method: 提出渐进式传感器事件扩展（PSEE），结合语义激活、传感器特定的过渡证据以及自适应时间归属，从点监督中恢复伪片段。这些伪片段用于监督标准TAL检测器，且不改变其推理过程。

Result: 在四个惯性传感基准上进行跨被试实验，相比适配的点监督基线，PSEE提升了伪边界质量，并兼容不同的TAL检测器，同时对点采样具有鲁棒性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: PSEE是一种有效的点监督TAL方法，通过恢复高质量伪片段降低标注成本，兼容现有检测器且鲁棒，适用于可穿戴传感器动作定位任务。

Abstract: Temporal action localization (TAL) in wearable sensor streams identifies action classes and temporal boundaries, enabling finer-grained activity understanding than conventional action recognition. However, training typically requires costly start--end annotations for every action instance. To reduce this burden, we study point-supervised TAL, where each instance is labeled with only one timestamp and its class. We propose Progressive Sensor Event Expansion (PSEE), which combines semantic activations, sensor-specific transition evidence, and adaptive temporal ownership to recover point-supervised pseudo segments. These segments supervise standard TAL detectors without modifying their inference procedures. Cross-subject experiments on four inertial-sensing benchmarks demonstrate improved pseudo-boundary quality over adapted point-supervised baselines, compatibility with different TAL detectors, and robustness to point sampling. Code is available at https://github.com/joeeeeyin/PSEE.

</details>


### [36] [SkillIR: Evolving Scene-Aware Skills for Agentic Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21468)
*Jie Shao,Shengkai Hu,Xu Zhang,Beihang Song,Yongcheng Jing,Xu Wu,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillIR，一种以技能引导的智能体图像复原框架，将复原经验表示为以退化为中心的动作证据而非完整工具使用轨迹，通过逐步验证的动作循环实现自适应复原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像复原智能体通常从原始退化图像直接推导完整工具使用计划，或检索此前成功的轨迹，难以为单个动作根据不断变化的中间复原状态进行自适应调整。作者发现被接受的工具执行会改变残余退化状态，从而影响后续工具的适用性。

Method: SkillIR将上下文相关的动作结果整合为场景感知的复原技能，刻画适用条件、预期效果与可归因失败案例。检索到的技能不预设完整复原计划，而是在经过验证的残余状态循环中每次引导一个有界动作：每个工具输出先作为候选，仅在转移验证通过后才被执行，随后重新评估当前残余退化。每次rollout后的证据用于创建、精炼或修补动态技能，使积累的经验改进后续输入的决策。

Result: 在合成和真实世界多重退化数据集上的实验表明，SkillIR提升了复原质量，并实现了更可靠、更有效的工具使用。

Conclusion: 将复原经验抽象为以退化为中心、可动态更新的技能，并配合逐步验证的动作循环，比依赖固定完整计划或轨迹检索更能适应复杂退化下的图像复原任务。

Abstract: This paper studies agentic image restoration, in which multimodal agents coordinate specialized restoration tools to recover images affected by complex degradations. Existing restoration agents often derive complete tool-use plans from the original degraded image or retrieve previously successful trajectories, providing limited support for adapting individual actions to evolving intermediate restoration states. We find that accepted tool executions can change the residual degradation state and, consequently, the applicability of subsequent tools. To address this issue, we propose SkillIR, a skill-guided framework that represents restoration experience as degradation-centered action evidence rather than complete tool-use trajectories. SkillIR consolidates context-dependent action outcomes into scene-aware restoration skills that characterize applicable conditions, expected effects, and attributable failure cases. Instead of prescribing a complete restoration plan, the retrieved skills guide one bounded action at a time within a verified residual-state loop: each tool output is treated as a candidate, committed only after transition verification, and followed by reassessment of the active residual degradations. After each rollout, the resulting evidence is used to create, refine, or patch dynamic skills, enabling accumulated restoration experience to improve decision-making for subsequent inputs. Experiments on synthetic and real-world multi-degradation datasets demonstrate that SkillIR improves restoration quality and enables more reliable and effective tool use.

</details>


### [37] [MT-WAM: Reorienting the One-Pass Predictive Representation Toward Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21474)
*Yiguang Yang,Jiankun Peng,Xiaoming Wang,Yiran Zhang,Zhibo Fang*

Main category: cs.CV
